**Scenario:** Charlie discovers strange footprints near the school garden. Something has been snacking on the lettuce, and Charlie wants to solve the mystery without blaming the class hamster again.

**Question:** What should Charlie do first?

**Choices:**

A. Look closely at the footprints **(correct)**
B. Look from the path and avoid disturbing clues
C. Blame the hamster
D. Step on the footprints

**Difficulty reason:** appropriate simplicity for K-1

**Improved distractors (suggested):**
- Choose a reasonable step that does not match the scenario best
- Pick a strategy that helps a little but misses the main goal
- Select an option that delays the decision
